{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nPi05 (π0.5) wrapper for ACR-VLA.\\n\\nProvides:\\n  1. obs_features extraction (mean-pooled VLM hidden states, 2048D)\\n  2. K-sample flow-matching generation with sequential denoising\\n  3. LIBERO data preprocessing (image, language, state → PI05 inputs)\\n\\nPreprocessing follows the official lerobot PI05 pipeline:\\n  - Images: resize_with_pad to 224×224 → float [0,1]\\n  - State: MEAN_STD normalize → discretize 256 bins → embed in text prompt\\n  - Action output: MEAN_STD unnormalize with mean/std stats\\n\\nKey differences from SmolVLA wrapper:\\n  - VLM backbone: PaliGemma 2B (hidden_size=2048) vs Idefics3 (960D)\\n  - State: embedded in text prompt (not as separate tensor)\\n  - Normalization: MEAN_STD\\n  - KV cache: HuggingFace DynamicCache (deep-copied per denoise step internally)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\nfrom collections import deque\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\\n\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import (\\n    PI05Policy,\\n    make_att_2d_masks,\\n    resize_with_pad_torch,\\n)\\n\\ntry:\\n    from utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    from .utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\n\\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\\n\\n\\ndef split_action(action_7d):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Split LIBERO 7D action into 6D pose and 1D gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return action_7d[..., :CORRECTION_DIM], action_7d[..., CORRECTION_DIM:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n\\n\\nclass Pi05Wrapper:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Frozen π0.5 for obs_features and K-sample generation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE = 2048\\n\\n    def __init__(self, pretrained_path: str = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\",\\n                 device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\n        # Open-loop action chunk queue. PI05 emits chunk_size=50 actions per\\n        # inference call; replaying them open-loop (1 inference per 50 env\\n        # steps) is the official lerobot contract. Re-running inference each\\n        # env step and only consuming action[0] starves multi-step behaviors\\n        # like the gripper-close at chunk step ~20, which produces 0% SR.\\n        self._action_queue = deque()\\n        self.policy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, strict=False)\\n        self._fix_vision_tower_keys(pretrained_path)\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\n        self.policy.eval()\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        self.model = self.policy.model  # PI05Pytorch\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE\\n\\n        # Tokenizer (PaliGemma / Gemma — same SentencePiece vocab)\\n        from transformers import AutoTokenizer\\n        _tok_candidates = [\\n            \\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\",\\n            \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer\\\",\\n            \\\"unsloth/gemma-2b\\\",\\n        ]\\n        self.tokenizer = None\\n        for _name in _tok_candidates:\\n            try:\\n                self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n                    _name, local_files_only=(_name == _tok_candidates[0])\\n                )\\n                break\\n            except Exception:\\n                continue\\n        if self.tokenizer is None:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Cannot load tokenizer from any of {_tok_candidates}\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer.padding_side = \\\"right\\\"\\n\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\n\\n        # Load normalizer stats (MEAN_STD)\\n        model_dir = Path(pretrained_path)\\n        self._load_normalizer_stats(model_dir)\\n\\n\\n    def _fix_vision_tower_keys(self, pretrained_path: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Fix vision tower key mismatch between saved weights and model architecture.\\n        \\n        Saved weights use: .vision_tower.vision_model.X\\n        Model expects:     .vision_tower.X  (transformers >= 5.x)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        sd = load_file(str(Path(pretrained_path) / \\\"model.safetensors\\\"))\\n        remap = {}\\n        for k, v in sd.items():\\n            if \\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\" in k:\\n                new_k = k.replace(\\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\", \\\"vision_tower.\\\")\\n                if not new_k.startswith(\\\"model.\\\"):\\n                    new_k = f\\\"model.{new_k}\\\"\\n                remap[new_k] = v\\n        if remap:\\n            missing, unexpected = self.policy.load_state_dict(remap, strict=False)\\n            loaded = len(remap) - len(unexpected)\\n            print(f\\\"[pi05] Fixed {loaded} vision tower keys\\\")\\n\\n    def _load_normalizer_stats(self, model_dir: Path):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Load MEAN_STD normalization stats (mean, std) from model directory.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        pre_files = sorted(model_dir.glob(\\\"*normalizer*processor*.safetensors\\\"))\\n        post_files = sorted(model_dir.glob(\\\"*unnormalizer*processor*.safetensors\\\"))\\n\\n        if pre_files:\\n            pre_stats = load_file(str(pre_files[0]))\\n            self.state_mean = pre_stats.get(\\n                \\\"observation.state.mean\\\", torch.zeros(32)\\n            ).to(self.device)\\n            self.state_std = pre_stats.get(\\n                \\\"observation.state.std\\\", torch.ones(32)\\n            ).to(self.device)\\n        else:\\n            self.state_mean = torch.zeros(32, device=self.device)\\n            self.state_std = torch.ones(32, device=self.device)\\n\\n        if post_files:\\n            post_stats = load_file(str(post_files[0]))\\n            self.action_mean = post_stats.get(\\n                \\\"action.mean\\\", torch.zeros(32)\\n            ).to(self.device)\\n            self.action_std = post_stats.get(\\n                \\\"action.std\\\", torch.ones(32)\\n            ).to(self.device)\\n        else:\\n            self.action_mean = torch.zeros(32, device=self.device)\\n            self.action_std = torch.ones(32, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def _preprocess_image(self, img_np: np.ndarray) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"HWC uint8 numpy → (1, C, H, W) float tensor ready for PI05.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        img = self._to_tensor(img_np)  # CHW float [0,1]\\n        img = img.unsqueeze(0).to(self.device)\\n        img = torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])\\n        h, w = self.config.image_resolution\\n        img = resize_with_pad_torch(img, h, w)\\n        img = img * 2.0 - 1.0  # [0,1] -> [-1,1] for SigLIP\\n        return img\\n\\n    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"MEAN_STD normalize state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\\n        mean = self.state_mean[:D]\\n        std = self.state_std[:D]\\n        denom = std + NORM_EPS\\n        return (state_raw - mean) / denom\\n\\n    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"MEAN_STD unnormalize action to original scale.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        mean = self.action_mean[:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n        std = self.action_std[:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n        return action_norm[..., :FULL_ACTION_DIM] * std + mean\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def preprocess_obs(self, agentview_rgb: np.ndarray,\\n                       task_name: str,\\n                       robot_state: np.ndarray,\\n                       eye_in_hand_rgb: np.ndarray | None = None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Convert raw LIBERO observation into PI05-ready tensors.\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def _preprocess_image(self, img_np: np.ndarray) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"HWC uint8 numpy → (1, C, H, W) float tensor ready for PI05.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        img = self._to_tensor(img_np)  # CHW float [0,1]\\n        img = img.unsqueeze(0).to(self.device)\\n        img = torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])\\n        h, w = self.config.image_resolution\\n        img = resize_with_pad_torch(img, h, w)\\n        img = img * 2.0 - 1.0  # [0,1] -> [-1,1] for SigLIP\\n        return img\\n\\n    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"MEAN_STD normalize state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\\n        mean = self.state_mean[:D]\\n        std = self.state_std[:D]\\n        denom = std + NORM_EPS\\n        return (state_raw - mean) / denom\\n\\n    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"MEAN_STD unnormalize action to original scale.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        mean = self.action_mean[:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n        std = self.action_std[:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n        return action_norm[..., :FULL_ACTION_DIM] * std + mean\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def preprocess_obs(self, agentview_rgb: np.ndarray,\\n                       task_name: str,\\n                       robot_state: np.ndarray,\\n {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nPi05 (π0.5) wrapper for ACR-VLA.\\n\\nProvides:\\n  1. obs_features extraction (mean-pooled VLM hidden states, 2048D)\\n  2. K-sample flow-matching generation with sequential denoising\\n  3. LIBERO data preprocessing (image, language, state → PI05 inputs)\\n\\nPreprocessing follows the official lerobot PI05 pipeline:\\n  - Images: resize_with_pad to 224×224 → float [0,1]\\n  - State: MEAN_STD normalize → discretize 256 bins → embed in text prompt\\n  - Action output: MEAN_STD unnormalize with mean/std stats\\n\\nKey differences from SmolVLA wrapper:\\n  - VLM backbone: PaliGemma 2B (hidden_size=2048) vs Idefics3 (960D)\\n  - State: embedded in text prompt (not as separate tensor)\\n  - Normalization: MEAN_STD\\n  - KV cache: HuggingFace DynamicCache (deep-copied per denoise step internally)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\nfrom collections import deque\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\\n\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import (\\n    PI05Policy,\\n    make_att_2d_masks,\\n    resize_with_pad_torch,\\n)\\n\\ntry:\\n    from utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    from .utils import FULL_ACTION_DIM, CORRECTION_DIM\\n\\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\\n\\n\\ndef split_action(action_7d):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Split LIBERO 7D action into 6D pose and 1D gripper.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return action_7d[..., :CORRECTION_DIM], action_7d[..., CORRECTION_DIM:FULL_ACTION_DIM]\\n\\n\\nclass Pi05Wrapper:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Frozen π0.5 for obs_features and K-sample generation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE = 2048\\n\\n    def __init__(self, pretrained_path: str = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\",\\n                 device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\n        # Open-loop action chunk queue. PI05 emits chunk_size=50 actions per\\n        # inference call; replaying them open-loop (1 inference per 50 env\\n        # steps) is the official lerobot contract. Re-running inference each\\n        # env step and only consuming action[0] starves multi-step behaviors\\n        # like the gripper-close at chunk step ~20, which produces 0% SR.\\n        self._action_queue = deque()\\n        self.policy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, strict=False)\\n        self._fix_vision_tower_keys(pretrained_path)\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\n        self.policy.eval()\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        self.model = self.policy.model  # PI05Pytorch\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.VLM_HIDDEN_SIZE\\n\\n        # Tokenizer (PaliGemma / Gemma — same SentencePiece vocab)\\n        from transformers import AutoTokenizer\\n        _tok_candidates = [\\n            \\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\",\\n            \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer\\\",\\n            \\\"unsloth/gemma-2b\\\",\\n        ]\\n        self.tokenizer = None\\n        for _name in _tok_candidates:\\n            try:\\n                self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n                    _name, local_files_only=(_name == _tok_candidates[0])\\n                )\\n                break\\n            except Exception:\\n                continue\\n        if self.tokenizer is None:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"Cannot load tokenizer from any of {_tok_candidates}\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer.padding_side = \\\"right\\\"\\n\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\n\\n        # Load normalizer stats (MEAN_STD)\\n        model_dir = Path(pretrained_path)\\n        self._load_normalizer_stats(model_dir)\\n\\n\\n    def _fix_vision_tower_keys(self, pretrained_path: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Fix vision tower key mismatch between saved weights and model architecture.\\n        \\n        Saved weights use: .vision_tower.vision_model.X\\n        Model expects:     .vision_tower.X  (transformers >= 5.x)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        sd = load_file(str(Path(pretrained_path) / \\\"model.safetensors\\\"))\\n        remap = {}\\n        for k, v in sd.items():\\n            if \\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\" in k:\\n                new_k = k.replace(\\\"vision_tower.vision_model.\\\", \\\"vision_tower.\\\")\\n                if not new_k.startswith(\\\"model.\\\"):\\n                    new_k = f\\\"model.{new_k}\\\"\\n                remap[new_k] = v\\n        if remap:\\n            missing, unexpected = self.policy.load_state_dict(remap, strict=False)\\n            loaded = len(remap) - len(unexpected)\\n            print(f\\\"[pi05] Fixed {loaded} vision tower keys\\\")\\n            if missing:\\n                print(f\\\"[pi05] Vision tower missing_keys ({len(missing)}): {missing[:5]}\\\")\\n            if unexpected:\\n                print(f\\\"[pi05] Vision tower unexpected_keys ({len(unexpected)}): {unexpected[:5]}\\\")\\n\\n    def _load_normalizer_stats(self, model_dir: Path):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Load MEAN_STD normalization stats (mean, std) from model directory.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        pre_files = sorted(model_dir.glob(\\\"*normalizer*processor*.safetensors\\\"))\\n        post_files = sorted(model_dir.glob(\\\"*unnormalizer*processor*.safetensors\\\"))\\n\\n        if pre_files:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}

### Assistant

_source line 69_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Official code flips both H and W dimensions with `torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])` on NCHW tensors, and this is done BEFORE any other image processing. In our wrapper, the flip is at line 154:

```python
def _preprocess_image(self, img_np: np.ndarray) -> torch.Tensor:
    img = self._to_tensor(img_np)  # CHW float [0,1]
    img = img.unsqueeze(0).to(self.device)


```

Then I apply the 180° flip to match LIBERO's convention, resize the image with padding to the configured resolution, and normalize the pixel values from [0,1] to [-1,1] for the SigLIP model. This ordering—flip first, then resize, then normalize—matches how the official implementation handles preprocessing.

Now I'm setting up a quick verification command to test the evaluation pipeline. I'll run it in vla_only mode with K=1 to use the simplest open-loop chunk replay approach, but I need to add a task filter since without it the script will try to load all 10 object tasks instead of just one.

Actually, running all 10 tasks with 3 rollouts each would still be manageable—that's 30 total rollouts. Given that PI05 uses chunk-based inference with 50-step chunks, each rollout could take a few seconds, so the whole evaluation might run in 10-20 minutes depending on how long each inference takes.

For a quicker sanity check though, I'll just run a single task with 3 rollouts and use the task filter to isolate it. Let me see which object task comes first.
````

_source line 165_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
No error in the log. It just stops at "Loading PI05 from ...". The process was killed (likely OOM). The RSS was 14.5GB. Let me check if this is a RAM OOM or CUDA OOM. Since GPU usage never went above 3MiB, it's definitely a CPU RAM OOM.

The problem is that `Pi05Wrapper.__init__` calls:
1. `PI05Policy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, strict=False)` - loads the full 7.5GB model into CPU
2. `_fix_vision_tower_keys` - loads 7.5GB safetensors AGAIN into CPU just to get the vision tower keys
   
So we're looking at 15GB+ of CPU RAM just during the loading phase, before accounting for the overhead from safetensors parsing and model initialization. I need to check how much RAM is actually available on this machine.
```

(H_eff, 6)\\n\\n    triggered = uncertainty > conformal_threshold\\n\\n    if triggered:\\n        base_action = selected_pose.reshape(1, -1).to(device)  # (1, 30)\\n        obs_feat_dev = obs_feat.to(device)\\n        if obs_proj is not None:\\n            obs_feat_dev = obs_proj(obs_feat_dev)\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            refined_pose = crm(base_action, None, obs_feat_dev)\\n        refined_pose = refined_pose[0].reshape(H_EFF, CORRECTION_DIM)\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\n        action_full = torch.cat([refined_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\n    else:\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\n        action_full = torch.cat([selected_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\n\\n    step_metadata.append({\\n        \\\"uncertainty\\\": uncertainty,\\n        \\\"triggered\\\": triggered,\\n    })\\n    return action_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef rollout_single(env, init_state, action_fn, max_steps=300, wrapper=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run one rollout. action_fn returns 7D action directly.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    env.reset()\\n    # Chunk-emitting wrappers (PI05) cache an action chunk across calls; flush\\n    # it here so a new rollout starts with a fresh inference.\\n    if wrapper is not None and hasattr(wrapper, \\\"reset_action_queue\\\"):\\n        wrapper.reset_action_queue()\\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\\n    for _ in range(5):\\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.zeros(7))\\n\\n    for step in range(max_steps):\\n        try:\\n            action_7d = action_fn(obs)\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action_7d)\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            print(f\\\"[rollout] Exception at step {step}: {e}\\\", flush=True)\\n            traceback.print_exc()\\n            return False, step\\n\\n        if done or info.get(\\\"success\\\", False):\\n            return True, step + 1\\n\\n    return False, max_steps\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(args):\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n\\n    # --crm_checkpoint is an alias for --checkpoint\\n    if args.crm_checkpoint and not args.checkpoint:\\n        args.checkpoint = args.crm_checkpoint\\n\\n    # Default checkpoint for adaptive_k mode\\n    if args.mode in (\\\"adaptive_k\\\", \\\"adaptive_unc_head\\\", \\\"adaptive_conformal_crm\\\") and args.checkpoint is None:\\n        args.checkpoint = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/crm_v3_norm/obs_only/crm_best.pt\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"[eval] {args.mode}: using default CRM checkpoint {args.checkpoint}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"[eval] Mode: {args.mode}, benchmark: {args.benchmark}\\\")\\n\\n    # Load VLA model\\n    if args.model == \\\"pi05\\\":\\n        from pi05_wrapper import Pi05Wrapper\\n        print(f\\\"[eval] Loading PI05 from {args.pi05_path}...\\\")\\n        wrapper = Pi05Wrapper(args.pi05_path, device=str(device))\\n    else:\\n        from smolvla_wrapper import SmolVLAWrapper\\n        print(f\\\"[eval] Loading SmolVLA from {args.smolvla_path}...\\\")\\n        wrapper = SmolVLAWrapper(args.smolvla_path, device=str(device))\\n\\n    # CGS — operates on 6D pose only (CORRECTION_DIM)\\n    cgs = ConformilizedGaussianScoring(K=args.K, horizon=H_EFF, action_dim=CORRECTION_DIM)\\n\\n    all_adaptive_meta = []\\n    all_unc_head_meta = []\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}

### Assistant

_source line 272_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The wrapper is still being constructed. The RSS was 12GB on CPU + 9.5GB on GPU. The process is still running. 

The tokenizer loading with `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` and `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` might be slow since it tries to load from `google/paligemma-3b-pt-224` with `local_files_only=True`, which would fail since it's not local, then tries `/root/autodl-tmp/models/gemma-2b-tokenizer` (which would need to exist), then tries `unsloth/gemma-2b` (also with online access disabled).
 
Actually, those environment variables are only set in evaluate.py, not when running the wrapper directly, so the tokenizer will attempt to download from HuggingFace instead. Given the server is hosted on seetacloud.com, a Chinese cloud provider, network latency could be slowing things down.

Looking at the tokenizer fallback chain in the wrapper: the first option uses `local_files_only=True` which would fail immediately if not cached, the second points to a local path that may or may not exist, and the third would try to download from the hub.
```

</details>

### Assistant

_source line 336_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
There's the problem! The cgroup memory limit is 64424509440 bytes = 60 GB. But the `from_pretrained` loads a 7.5GB model, then `_fix_vision_tower_keys` loads another 7.5GB safetensors. With PyTorch's model initialization, tensor copies during `load_state_dict`, the tokenizer, and other imports, we easily exceed 60GB.

Let me think about how to fix this:

1. The main waste is in `_fix_vision_tower_keys` loading the ENTIRE 7.5GB safetensors just to extract vision tower keys (~100MB).


2. We could instead extract just the vision tower keys more efficiently.

But actually, `from_pretrained` itself probably uses 15-22GB during loading due to HuggingFace's intermediate copies—a 7.5GB model can balloon to 2-3x its size during initialization. Then `_fix_vision_tower_keys` adds another 7.5GB on top of that, which would push us well over the 60GB limit.

Looking at the actual memory breakdown during rollout: the model on GPU takes ~9.5GB, the CPU copy stays around 7.5GB, the safetensors file in `_fix_vision_tower_keys` is another 7.5GB, plus Python overhead and the LIBERO environment. My import test showed RSS at 14.5GB and GPU at 9.5GB (24GB total), which should fit, but evaluate.py keeps dying at inconsistent stages—sometimes after loading, sometimes during imports, sometimes during model initialization—which suggests something else is consuming memory or there's a process leak I'm not catching.
```

</details>
